47 /**
48  * S3 object store implementation
49  *
50  * @param client s3 client
51  * @param buffer write buffering strategy
52  */
53 class S3ObjectStore(val client: S3AsyncClient, buffer: S3WriteBuffering) extends ObjectStore {
54 
55   import S3ObjectStore.parseS3Path
56 
57   import scala.collection.JavaConverters._
58 
59   private val transferManager = S3TransferManager.builder().s3Client(client).build()
60 
61   // accelerated input stream for working with parquet files
62   val seekableInputStreamFactory: S3SeekableInputStreamFactory =
63     new S3SeekableInputStreamFactory(new S3SdkObjectClient(client, false), S3SeekableInputStreamConfiguration.DEFAULT)
64 
65   override val scheme: String = "s3"
66 
67   override def exists(path: URI): Boolean = {
68     val key = parseS3Path(path)
69     val request = HeadObjectRequest.builder().bucket(key.bucket).key(key.key).build()
70     client.headObject(request).handle[Option[Boolean]](ifFound(_ => true)).join().getOrElse(false)
71   }
72 
73   override def size(path: URI): Long = {
74     val key = parseS3Path(path)
75     val request =
76       GetObjectAttributesRequest.builder().bucket(key.bucket).key(key.key).objectAttributes(ObjectAttributes.OBJECT_SIZE).build()
77     client.getObjectAttributes(request).handle[Option[Long]](ifFound(_.objectSize())).join().getOrElse(0L)
78   }
79 
80   override def modified(path: URI): Option[Long] = {
81     val key = parseS3Path(path)
82     val request = HeadObjectRequest.builder().bucket(key.bucket).key(key.key).build()
83     client.headObject(request).handle[Option[Long]](ifFound(_.lastModified().toEpochMilli)).join()
84   }
85 
86   // note: since the write is not finalized until it is uploaded, this may return a Some but then throw an exception on close
87   // if the path is written to after the existence check
88   override def create(path: URI): Option[OutputStream] = {
89     if (exists(path)) { None } else {
90       val key = parseS3Path(path)
91       Some(buffer.write(key.bucket, key.key, overwrite = false))
92     }
93   }
94 
95   override def overwrite(path: URI): OutputStream = {
96     val key = parseS3Path(path)
97     buffer.write(key.bucket, key.key, overwrite = true)
98   }
99 
100   override def read(path: URI): Option[InputStream] = {
101     val key = parseS3Path(path)
102     val request =
103       DownloadRequest.builder()
104         .getObjectRequest(GetObjectRequest.builder().bucket(key.bucket).key(key.key).build())
105         .responseTransformer(AsyncResponseTransformer.toBlockingInputStream())
106         .build()
107     transferManager.download(request).completionFuture()
108       .handle[Option[InputStream]](ifFound(r => PathUtils.handleCompression(r.result(), path.toString)))
109       .join()
110   }
111 
112   override def read(path: URI, format: ArchiveFormat): CloseableIterator[NamedInputStream] = {
113     val iter = format match {
114       case ArchiveFormat.Tar =>
115         CloseableIterator(read(path).iterator).flatMap { is =>
116           val archive: ArchiveInputStream[_ <: ArchiveEntry] =
117             S3ObjectStore.archiveFactory.createArchiveInputStream(ArchiveStreamFactory.TAR, is)
118           new ArchiveFileIterator(archive, path.toString)
119         }
120 
121       case ArchiveFormat.Zip =>
122         // we have to download the file to get random access reads
123         // note: there is a potential way to wrap the request in a Seekable Channel using
124         // https://github.com/awslabs/aws-java-nio-spi-for-s3/, but it doesn't seem worthwhile assuming
125         // we end up reading the whole file
126         val name = filename(path)
127         val tmp = Files.createTempFile(FilenameUtils.removeExtension(name), FilenameUtils.getExtension(name))
128         CloseableIterator(read(path).iterator).flatMap { is =>
129           WithClose(is)(IOUtils.copy(_, new FileOutputStream(tmp.toFile)))
130           val zipIter = new ZipFileIterator(new ZipFile(tmp), path.toString)
131           new CloseableIterator[(String, InputStream)]() {
132             override def hasNext: Boolean = zipIter.hasNext
133             override def next(): (String, InputStream) = zipIter.next()
134             override def close(): Unit = {
135               try { zipIter.close() } finally {
136                 Files.delete(tmp)
137               }
138             }
139           }
140         }
141       case _ =>
142         throw new UnsupportedOperationException(s"An implementation is missing for format $format")
143     }
144     iter.map { case (name, is) => NamedInputStream(name, is) }
145   }
146 
147   override def list(path: URI): CloseableIterator[URI] = {
148     val key = parseS3Path(path)
149     // ensure prefix ends with '/' to target only items inside it
150     val prefix = if (key.key.endsWith("/")) { key.key } else { key.key + "/" }
151     list(path.getScheme, key.bucket, prefix, null)
152   }
153 
154   private def list(scheme: String, bucket: String, prefix: String, continuation: String): CloseableIterator[URI] = {
155     val request =
156       ListObjectsV2Request.builder()
157         .bucket(bucket)
158         .prefix(prefix)
159         .delimiter("/") // prevents listing more than 1 directory deep
160         .continuationToken(continuation)
161         .build()
162     val response = client.listObjectsV2(request).join()
163     val files = response.contents().asScala.map(_.key())
164     val folders = response.commonPrefixes().asScala.map(_.prefix())
165     val results = CloseableIterator.wrap(files ++ folders).map(key => new URI(s"$scheme://$bucket/$key"))
166     if (response.isTruncated) {
167       results.concat(list(scheme, bucket, prefix, response.continuationToken()))
168     } else {
169       results
170     }
171   }
172 
173   override def copy(from: URI, to: URI): Unit = {
174     val fromKey = parseS3Path(from)
175     val toKey = parseS3Path(to)
176     val request =
177       CopyObjectRequest.builder()
178         .sourceBucket(fromKey.bucket)
179         .sourceKey(fromKey.key)
180         .destinationBucket(toKey.bucket)
181         .destinationKey(toKey.key)
182         .build()
183     client.copyObject(request).join()
184   }
185 
186   override def delete(path: URI): Unit = {
187     val key = parseS3Path(path)
188     val request =
189       DeleteObjectRequest.builder()
190         .bucket(key.bucket)
191         .key(key.key)
192         .build()
193     client.deleteObject(request).join()
194   }
195 
196   override def close(): Unit = CloseWithLogging(client, transferManager)
197 
198   /**
199    * Helper method to handle missing key errors in completable futures
200    *
201    * @param resultTransform success result
202    * @return
203    */
204   private def ifFound[T, U](resultTransform: T => U): BiFunction[T, Throwable, Option[U]] = { (result, error) =>
205     if (error == null) {
206       Option(resultTransform(result))
207     } else {
208       val unwrapped = error match {
209         case e: CompletionException => e.getCause
210         case _ => error
211       }
212       unwrapped match {
213         case e: S3Exception if e.statusCode() == 404 => None
214         case _: NoSuchKeyException => None
215         case _ => throw error
216       }
217     }
218   }
219 }
220 
221 object S3ObjectStore {
222 
223   val Type = "s3"
224 
225   private val archiveFactory = new ArchiveStreamFactory()
226 
227   def parseS3Path(path: URI): S3Path = {
228     if (path.getScheme != "s3" && path.getScheme != "s3a") {
229       throw new UnsupportedOperationException(s"Trying to use S3 to operate on a non-s3 URI: $path")
230     }
231     S3Path(path.getHost, path.getPath.stripPrefix("/"))
232   }
233 
234   /**
235    * Create a new S3 store
236    *
237    * @param conf config
238    * @return
239    */
240   def apply(conf: Map[String, String]): S3ObjectStore = {
241     val config = S3ObjectStoreConfig(conf)
242     val bufferSize = org.locationtech.geomesa.utils.text.Suffixes.Memory.bytes(config.writeBufferInBytes).get
243     if (bufferSize > Int.MaxValue) {
244       throw new IllegalArgumentException(s"Buffer size exceeds max size in bytes (${Int.MaxValue}b): $bufferSize")
245     }
246 
247     val builder = S3AsyncClient.crtBuilder()
248     // or AWS_REGION
249     config.region.foreach(builder.region)
250     // endpoint override (for MinIO, LocalStack, or other S3-compatible services)
251     config.endpoint.foreach(builder.endpointOverride)
252     // AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID/AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, or ~/.aws/credentials
253     config.accessKeyId.foreach { accessKeyId =>
254       config.secretAccessKey.foreach { secretAccessKey =>
255         builder.credentialsProvider(StaticCredentialsProvider.create(AwsBasicCredentials.create(accessKeyId, secretAccessKey)))
256       }
257     }
258     if (config.forcePathStyle) {
259       builder.forcePathStyle(true) // path-style access, often needed for MinIO/LocalStack
260     }
261 
262     config.numRetries.foreach(m => builder.retryConfiguration(c => c.numRetries(m)))
263     config.targetThroughputInGbps.foreach(builder.targetThroughputInGbps(_))
264     config.minimumPartSizeInBytes.foreach(builder.minimumPartSizeInBytes(_))
265     config.maxConcurrency.foreach(builder.maxConcurrency(_))
266     config.connectionTimeout.foreach(t => builder.httpConfiguration(c => c.connectionTimeout(t)))
267     config.maxNativeMemoryLimitInBytes.foreach(builder.maxNativeMemoryLimitInBytes(_))
268     config.requestChecksumCalculation.foreach(builder.requestChecksumCalculation)
269     config.responseChecksumValidation.foreach(builder.responseChecksumValidation)
270     config.initialReadBufferSizeInBytes.foreach(builder.initialReadBufferSizeInBytes(_))
271     config.accelerate.foreach(builder.accelerate(_))
272     config.thresholdInBytes.foreach(builder.thresholdInBytes(_))
273 
274     val client = builder.build()
275 
276     val buffering = try {
277       config.writeBuffering match {
278         case WriteBuffering.Disk => new DiskBuffering(client, bufferSize.toInt, new File(config.writeBufferDir))
279         case WriteBuffering.Memory => new MemoryBuffering(client, bufferSize.toInt)
280       }
281     } catch {
282       case NonFatal(e) => client.close(); throw e
283     }
284 
285     new S3ObjectStore(client, buffering)
286   }
287 
288   /**
289    * Converts geomesa s3-related configuration values to hadoop s3a ones
290    *
291    * @param conf geomesa configuration
292    * @return
293    */
294   def s3aConfigs(conf: Map[String, String]): Map[String, String] =
295     conf.flatMap { case (k, v) => S3ObjectStoreConfig.s3aReverseConfigMappings.get(k).map(_ -> v )}
296 
297   /**
298    * If the URI has an 's3://' scheme, converts it to 's3a://' for use with hadoop
299    *
300    * @param path path
301    * @return
302    */
303   def s3aUri(path: URI): URI = {
304     if (path.getScheme != "s3") { path } else {
305       new URI("s3a", path.getHost, path.getPath, path.getFragment)
306     }
307   }
308 
309   /**
310    * Write buffering strategies
311    */
312   object WriteBuffering extends Enumeration {
313     type WriteBuffering = Value
314     val Disk, Memory = Value
315   }
316 
317   case class S3Path(bucket: String, key: String)
318 
319   private case class S3ObjectStoreConfig(
320     region: Option[Region],
321     endpoint: Option[URI],
322     accessKeyId: Option[String],
323     secretAccessKey: Option[String],
324     forcePathStyle: Boolean,
325     numRetries: Option[Int],
326     targetThroughputInGbps: Option[Double],
327     minimumPartSizeInBytes: Option[Long],
328     maxConcurrency: Option[Int],
329     connectionTimeout: Option[Duration],
330     maxNativeMemoryLimitInBytes: Option[Long],
331     requestChecksumCalculation: Option[RequestChecksumCalculation],
332     responseChecksumValidation: Option[ResponseChecksumValidation],
333     initialReadBufferSizeInBytes: Option[Long],
334     accelerate: Option[Boolean],
335     thresholdInBytes: Option[Long],
336     writeBuffering: WriteBuffering,
337     writeBufferDir: String,
338     writeBufferInBytes: String,
339   )
340 
341   private object S3ObjectStoreConfig extends LazyLogging {
342 
343     import pureconfig.generic.auto._
344 
345     import scala.collection.JavaConverters._
346 
347     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
348     private implicit val productHint: ProductHint[S3ObjectStoreConfig] = {
349       val baseMapping = ConfigFieldMapping(CamelCase, KebabCase).withOverrides("s3Endpoint" -> "s3-endpoint")
350       val mapping = ConfigFieldMapping(s => "fs.s3." + baseMapping(s))
351       ProductHint[S3ObjectStoreConfig](mapping)
352     }
353 
354     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
355     private implicit val awsRegionConfigReader: ConfigReader[Region] =
356       ConfigReader.fromString { s =>
357         Try(Region.of(s)).toEither
358           .left.map(_ => CannotConvert(s, "Region", s"Must be one of: ${Region.regions().asScala.mkString(", ")}"))
359       }
360 
361     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
362     private implicit val retryModeConfigReader: ConfigReader[RetryMode] =
363       enumReader(classOf[RetryMode])
364 
365     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
366     private implicit val requestChecksumCalculationConfigReader: ConfigReader[RequestChecksumCalculation] =
367       enumReader(classOf[RequestChecksumCalculation])
368 
369     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
370     private implicit val responseChecksumCalculationConfigReader: ConfigReader[ResponseChecksumValidation] =
371       enumReader(classOf[ResponseChecksumValidation])
372 
373     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
374     private implicit val javaDurationConfigReader: ConfigReader[Duration] =
375       ConfigReader.fromNonEmptyString[Duration](s =>
376         Try(Duration.ofMillis(scala.concurrent.duration.Duration(s).toMillis))
377           .toEither.left.map(e => CannotConvert(s, classOf[Duration].getName, e.toString)))
378 
379     // noinspection ScalaUnusedSymbol
380     private implicit val writeBufferingReader: ConfigReader[WriteBuffering] =
381       ConfigReader.fromString { s =>
382         WriteBuffering.values.find(_.toString.equalsIgnoreCase(s)).toRight {
383           CannotConvert(s, "WriteBuffering", s"Must be one of: ${WriteBuffering.values.mkString(", ")}")
384         }
385       }
386 
387     private def enumReader[T <: Enum[T]](clas: Class[T]): ConfigReader[T] = {
388       ConfigReader.fromString { s =>
389         Try(Enum.valueOf[T](clas, s.toUpperCase(Locale.US))).toEither
390           .left.map(_ => CannotConvert(s, clas.getSimpleName, s"Must be one of: ${clas.getEnumConstants.mkString(", ")}"))
391       }
392     }
393 
394     private val s3aConfigMappings = Map(
395       "fs.s3a.access.key"         -> "fs.s3.access-key-id",
396       "fs.s3a.secret.key"         -> "fs.s3.secret-access-key",
397       "fs.s3a.endpoint"           -> "fs.s3.endpoint",
398       "fs.s3a.endpoint.region"    -> "fs.s3.region",
399       "fs.s3a.path.style.access"  -> "fs.s3.force-path-style",
400       "fs.s3a.attempts.maximum"   -> "fs.s3.num-retries",
401       "fs.s3a.connection.maximum" -> "fs.s3.max-concurrency", // TODO think max-concurrency is per-request
402       "fs.s3a.connection.timeout" -> "fs.s3.connection-timeout",
403       "fs.s3a.multipart.size"     -> "fs.s3.write-buffer-in-bytes",
404       "fs.s3a.buffer.dir"         -> "fs.s3.write-buffer-dir"
405     )
406 
407     val s3aReverseConfigMappings: Map[String, String] = s3aConfigMappings.map { case (k, v) => v -> k }
408 
409     def apply(conf: Map[String, String]): S3ObjectStoreConfig = {
410       val s3 = conf.flatMap { case (k, v) => s3aConfigMappings.get(k).map(_ -> v) }.asJava
411       val configSource =
412         ConfigValueFactory.fromMap(conf.asJava).toConfig
413           .withFallback(ConfigFactory.load())
414           .withFallback(ConfigValueFactory.fromMap(s3))
415           .withFallback(ConfigFactory.load("s3-defaults"))
416           .resolve()
417       val config = ConfigSource.fromConfig(configSource).loadOrThrow[S3ObjectStoreConfig]
418       logger.debug(
419         s"""S3 client configuration:
420            |  region=${config.region.getOrElse("")}
421            |  endpoint=${config.endpoint.getOrElse("")}
422            |  accessKeyId=${config.accessKeyId.fold("")(_ => "***")}
423            |  secretAccessKey=${config.secretAccessKey.fold("")(_ => "***")}
424            |  forcePathStyle=${config.forcePathStyle}
425            |  numRetries=${config.numRetries.getOrElse("")}
426            |  targetThroughputInGbps=${config.targetThroughputInGbps.getOrElse("")}
427            |  minimumPartSizeInBytes=${config.minimumPartSizeInBytes.getOrElse("")}
428            |  maxConcurrency=${config.maxConcurrency.getOrElse("")}
429            |  connectionTimeout=${config.connectionTimeout.getOrElse("")}
430            |  maxNativeMemoryLimitInBytes=${config.maxNativeMemoryLimitInBytes.getOrElse("")}
431            |  requestChecksumCalculation=${config.requestChecksumCalculation.getOrElse("")}
432            |  responseChecksumValidation=${config.responseChecksumValidation.getOrElse("")}
433            |  initialReadBufferSizeInBytes=${config.initialReadBufferSizeInBytes.getOrElse("")}
434            |  accelerate=${config.accelerate.getOrElse("")}
435            |  thresholdInBytes=${config.thresholdInBytes.getOrElse("")}
436            |  writeBuffering=${config.writeBuffering}
437            |  writeBufferDir=${config.writeBufferDir}
438            |  writeBufferInBytes=${config.writeBufferInBytes}""".stripMargin
439       )
440       config
441     }
442   }
